## EDIT WITH COPILOT IS A PAGE, NOT A DRAWER (owner-approved prototype 25 Aug 2026; the middle became the contract 26 Aug; Work Mode Prototype 28 Aug)

js/views/clauseeditor.js: the whole window goes to one clause, with Copilot down a THIRD of the screen (`minmax(0,2fr) minmax(340px,1fr)`); below 1024px the door is not offered (`clauseEditorFits`). Mounted on demand, removed on close; z-index **38** (below the drawers since 2 Sep — a panel covers it exactly as any page); it covers the PAGE, not the shell (`ceFitToShell` measures `#content-scroll`'s rect; a zero is refused).
- **THE RAIL RUNS FLOOR TO CEILING**: anything full-width goes in the LEFT column (a strip above `.ce-grid` pushes the rail down by its own height — pinned as geometry, 2d2/2d3). The header is gone (the prototype opens on the tool strip; the contract begins at 92px); `#ce-say` on the strip is where a refusal is spoken.
- **THE MIDDLE IS THE PRODUCT'S OWN CANVAS**: `redlineDocHtml` wrapped in `.redline-page` / `.rl-doc` / `.nego-scroll`. The seam for an unfiled draft is a BODY, not a diff — `opts.live = {clauseId, html}` for ONE clause, never persisted, never reachable by the counterparty. The three readings are `rlReadSegsHtml` in its third home; the draft answers the reading too (As agreed = today's words; With changes = the draft plain). `ceEditableReading()` is asked in three places (the pencil stands down, the caret stands down, `ceApply` REFUSES; Undo/Discard/File grey with the reason); `rlReadNoticeHtml` draws its one-line band with the way back on this surface only (`opts.on`), nothing on Redlined. No queue rail. The reading at OPEN is remembered and closing repaints the page below only if it moved. `rlReadMode` and its siblings are PUBLISHED (f232 caught it). `.redline-page` joined the `--n-ins-*` / `--n-del-*` token list.
- **APPLY IS THE ONLY THING THAT MOVES THE WORDING, INTO THE BOX**; it stacks; nothing files until the one act in the rail's foot. The redline is the product's own engine (redlineOps / redlineOpsBlocksHtml / redlineStats; f245 greps for hand-written marks). It FILES THROUGH negoEditClause AND NOTHING ELSE (f245 fails on negoFileChange/changes.push/negoInsertClause/negoDeleteClause in the file). Filing never closes the page (30 Aug): `ceSeedDraft` has two callers, the door and the filing; the File button asks `clauseEditorDirty`.
- **TWO DOORS, ONE ROUTE** — the panel's Copilot button (`data-rl-cp-editor`, keeping `data-nego-ai-clause` as fallback) and the card's Edit — both through `rlOpenClauseEditor` (NOT `openClauseEditor`, which settings.js owns). The way back is a `.ui-btn` at the head's right, dressed like `#ws-to-nego` (the old Close replaced); **since 10 Sep it SAYS "Exit" beside its symbol** (the only way out of a full-window page may not be a guess; height untouched at 28). The contract head draws NO second door onto the negotiation and NO Key terms door (all three `review-changes` branches and the key-terms branch take `noButton`, never null — the guide stays, the phone's bar stays true, `focusKeyTerms` stays wired).
- **THE CLAUSE YOU ARE TYPING IN IS STILL THE PAPER**: no fill, no ring — `outline:1px dashed` at 22% of the document's own ink, one frame round the clause; the green margin bar is deleted; the red changed-clause bar stays. No CHG chips, no "+ Something of my own" (Discard is that), no "On this clause" line (1 Sep).
- **TWO LISTS, TWO VERBS**: `ceScanGroups` splits `here` (may be edited in place) from `missing` (may only be ADDED, through `rlFilePlaybookProposal`, plain wording, no redline against a clause it has nothing to do with; asked `!it.clauseId` FIRST); `ceScanItems` is built from the groups. Three named wordings — `preferred` / `fallback` (the library's) and `draft` (the model's) — with `rlPbWordingLabel` the ONE naming and `lead`/`leadKind` saying which is previewed. `AI_REDLINE_RULE(jurisdiction)` asks for wording, never a note about wording, and for the SMALLEST change on a deviation. `ceCostLine(from, to)` says what the press takes (redlineStats' own word count; null where nothing honest to say; label shade never amber; each verb's cost on its hover). `rlPbFindClause` refuses when unsure (`RL_PB_MATCH_MIN` 0.7, `RL_PB_MATCH_LEAD` 0.15; words matched as WORDS) and `landing` has three answers — edit · add · **unplaced** (drawn on neither list, no verb, refused by `rlFilePlaybookProposal`). `pbVerdictWords` is the plain core, `pbVerdictLine` its escaped dress. A scan with nothing to read says so on the card (`_ceScanErr`, "Run it again"). Copilot's playbook line is CLAUSE-scoped (`ceClauseDeviations`).
- **THE SIZE BOX IS THE WORDS, THE −/+ IS THE VIEW** (zoom, a percentage, per sitting, on `.ce-paperwrap`). The Changes tab is DELETED (`ceFiledList`, `ceChangesHtml`, `.ce-chg` stale; keys inert). The selection menu is not built; deleting is a button on the passage card. Renaming a heading and the front matter are built (see their sections).
- **THE COUNTERPARTY'S SEAT WAS PROVED UNTOUCHED** byte for byte and by pressing (clause-editor-verify section 11).
- **THE HEAD WEARS `.room-head` / `.room-id` / `.room-name` / `.room-facts`** so it is dressed by the same rules as the negotiation head; `letter-spacing:0` is the one restated declaration; the fold control is deleted.
Tests: f245, f148, f176, f232, clause-editor-verify, clause-door-verify, redline-verify.

## THE WRITING BAR, AND SAVE IN ONE PRESS (owner-asked 28 Aug 2026)

`richBarHtml` in js/richdoc.js beside the sanitiser; `RICH_BAR_TOOLS` is ONE list (`full:1` marks what the compact shelf omits); `richBarPress`, `richMarkSelection`, `richUnmark`, `richSizeAt`. The colour door is narrow: a fixed list of CLASS names on a span (`RICH_MARK_CLASSES`, `richSpanClassOk`, asked by `_stripAttrs` AND the span unwrap) — five inks, four highlights, ten sizes; GREEN AND RED ARE NOT ON THE LIST (the redline's own grammar); every rule is `var(--mk-token, #literal)`. The bar sits INSIDE `.ce-head` in the LEFT column. SAVE IS ONE PRESS: the reason step is gone from both filing paths (`ng_why_this_change`, `ng_file_change`, `ng_skip_no_reason`, `ng_back_to_wording`, `ce_reason_hint`, `data-nego-save/-skip/-back/-reason`, `data-ce-act="reason-*"` stale; `data-nego-next` and `data-ce-act="save"` keep their names); the Copilot proposal card keeps its optional reason; `why` still travels where written. A BULLET LIST DRAWS ITS BULLET (the Tailwind preflight sets list-style none; HaTi's sheet puts it back). UNDO pulls the box first, `ceBoxDirty` is what the button asks, `_ceRendering` fences the paper's write. A tool that cannot act GREYS with the reason (Undo, Redo and Exit excepted). Tests: f245 (17), clause-editor-verify 19.

## HIGHLIGHT A PASSAGE — IT GOES TO THE COPILOT RAIL (M-1, owner-chose Option A, 31 Aug 2026)

The strip is DELETED (`.ce-inline` and five functions; `ce_inline_ph/newline/cancel/replace/replace_title/where/suggested/about` stale; `ce_inline_shorten/firmer/plain/say_what/moved/cut/cut_all/cut_title` LIVE). The passage attaches directly above the ask box; the paper's mouseup answers ONLY for a press inside `#ce-doc`; attaching never takes the caret; the chips follow the scope (passage: three; clause: four; one row, one attribute, every chip presses `ceAsk`); the ask box's placeholder follows the scope; the card KNOWS what it was about (the passage recorded ON the answer card); Apply files nothing, the CUT still files through `ceFile` (not a second path); `ceReplacePassage` is the ONE reading of "put this in place of that", refusing across two sub-paragraphs, speaking through `ceSay`; `ceMarkHeld` keeps the passage lit on the paper (three nets against reaching the record, f245 (21)); manual replacement is typing in the contract. LETTING GO MEANS LETTING GO (N-4): `ceDetachPassage` releases the browser's selection where it is still ours; `ceSelectionRead` answers the passage OR the reason there is not one (across sub-paragraphs, words appear twice, struck-out words) and `ceSelection` wraps it; a click is not a refusal. `ceScrollToClause` lands rather than travels (N-2). Tests: f245 (16)(19)(22)(23), clause-editor-verify 18, 21–24.

## ONE PRESS REACHES TYPING AND THE STRIP / HIGHLIGHT AND TYPE (29–30 Aug 2026)

Typing and the strip were made live at once (the mouseup handler no longer returns on `ceIsTyping()`); the three doors pass `typing:true` through one `openEditor`. TWO PRESSES ARE THE CEILING: while the caret is in the box nothing is REBUILT (`keepView` means paint, do not rebuild; `ceStatHtml` is the one builder for `+N −N`; the rail foot is PATCHED never rewritten; the paper is rebuilt only where the sanitiser corrected the box); the writing bar acts on the HELD SENTENCE wherever the caret is; a press on a control is not the end of a drag. The two doors onto the clause panel are shut on our seat (`ceTakesIt`; the ⋯'s Copilot row stands down where the face has it; the panel is kept for the counterparty and under 1024px); the way back from an ACCEPTED decision is on the accepted change's own card. `ng_cp_edit_title` names the page the pencil opens. Tests: f245, f246, f100, clause-editor-verify, clause-door-verify 16d2–16d5 (driven with a real mouse; a probe may not scroll the paper to find its target).

## NOTES ARE ONE SYSTEM — THREE DOORS, ONE DRAWER, AND THE THREAD GOES OUT TO WORD (Young asked 11 Sep 2026)

A note is ONE THING: a message on a thread, with an `id`, optionally an `anchor` {clauseId, quote} (the exact words it is about), optionally a `replyTo`, optionally `done` {at, by}. All four absent on every note on file. `negoNoteHomeFor(c, clauseId)` is the ONE reading of where an anchored note lives (the pending change on that clause, else the contract); `negoAnchorState` reads whether the words still stand (live · moved · gone · unknown — never negoInit); `negoNoteDone` is the one writer of done; `negoNoteThreads` groups roots and replies. The channel carries `meta` (id, anchor, replyTo, done; `msgMeta` is the server's allow-list) and the merge READS a twin's done onto ours. PATCH `/api/contracts/:id/messages/:mid` and `/api/shares/:token/messages/:mid` set done.
- THREE DOORS, ONE DRAWER: the highlight (`rlNoteFromSelection`), the pencil (`rlNoteAskAfterFile` → `openChangeNoteDialog` keeps its name and opens the drawer), the card's Notes row. **The receipt window is RETIRED** (`rlNoteDialogHtml` is a `return ''` stub; `#rl-note-overlay`, `.rl-note-dlg`, `#rl-note-in/-ok/-skip`, `data-rl-note-room` stale). The PIN (`rlNotesPin` / `rlNotesUnpin` / `rlNpPinFor`, per sitting) is what the drawer holds: the words or the change just filed, the room (Internal lit at rest, D-6), a draft per room, `resolve` for the filing doors; `openNotesPanel(cid, chId, {force:true})` opens without toggling; a closed drawer drops the pin (`rlNotesPanelClosed` from applyPanelLayout).
- REPLY IS ITS OWN ACT (its own box under the note, `data-rl-np-reply*`); the foot box only writes a NEW note; DONE folds the thread under "Done (n)"; EVERY NOTE PRINTS `negoWhenFull` (day through langLocale). `rlNpWireActs` arms them per paint on both panels; their seat has one room, no tabs.
- THE HIGHLIGHT OFFERS TWO THINGS on the negotiation paper and in the clause editor (`rlPaperSelOffer` / `ceOfferPassage`: Ask Copilot · Comment; their seat Comment alone; a preview nothing). The Document tab offers neither (untouched). Ask Copilot from the paper opens the editor with `opts.passage` → `ceAttachWords`. Copilot in the editor costs ONE press now.
- THE MARKS: `rlPaintNoteMarks` after every paint (negoAfterPaint leads with it; the editor's canvas calls it) — a numbered `.rl-note-mk` in the left gutter per OPEN anchored thread, `.rl-note-hl` on the words, hollow where moved, never inside the typing box; `[data-rl-note-open]` is delegated once. Their page has its own aside (`#pt-notes`, `portalOpenNotes`, a Notes row in More) drawing the same panel.
- WORD: `docxExportTracked(html, {comments})` writes comments.xml + commentsExtended.xml only where there is one (else byte-identical); `wordCommentsOf(c, side)` gathers the EXTERNAL room only, CHG first ("CHG-012: …"), replies threaded, done resolved; the import reads the prefix before it guesses by wording.
Tests: f303, f302 (re-pointed), f264 (4)(5), clause-editor-verify 25, window-drag-verify 5.

## COMMENTS, ROUND TWO — THREE VERBS, A PLAINER PIN, A MARKER THAT CLOSES (Young, 11 Sep 2026, evening)

Eleven reports off the first day with the comments system (WORKORDER-comments-round-two.md), built on "go".
- **AND IT IS A BAR, NOT A DROPDOWN (Young ruled 15 Sep 2026: *"I am still getting the Hati version and not the artifact version"*)**: the artifact's own words are *"the bar appears in place … It is a bar, not a dropdown."* `negoSelBarHtml(actions)` is the ONE markup builder — the room's `defaultMenu` and the workbench's `rlSelMenu` were two copies of one shape and both ask it now. The verbs sit SIDE BY SIDE (`.nego-selmenu.nego-selbar`, written AFTER the column rules it beats, at equal specificity, so it wins by ORDER); 13px, bold, `--accent-ink`, Copilot's two in Copilot's violet through `.rl-verb-ai`. **THE HEAD AND THE QUOTE ARE GONE** (`.nego-selhead` / `.nego-selquote` STALE on the bar; `ng_sel_*` lost their glyphs in both books): a label on a layer the reader's own drag just raised, and a copy of words that are lit twelve pixels below it. `_negoAnchor(rect, w, h, 'bar')` centres it OVER the highlight and clear of it; the refusal layer keeps `.nego-selmenu` alone, stays a column and keeps its head, because a refusal is a sentence to be read.
- **A HIGHLIGHT OFFERS THREE VERBS, NO PENCIL FIRST**: Ask Copilot · Edit with Copilot · Comment, on the negotiation paper (`rlPaperSelOffer`, the verb rides as `passageMode`) and in the editor (`ceOfferPassage`); their seat Comment alone. **THE VERB DECIDES WHETHER COPILOT MAY TOUCH THE WORDING**: `_ceSel.mode` — `'ask'` is a question (`ce_prompt_question`, question chips, no cut, no Apply anywhere, nothing recorded as proposed; the answer card carries **Edit with this** → `ceEditWith`, which re-attaches the same words under `'edit'` and turns held wording into a card with Apply); `'edit'` is the rail as before. `ceAttachPassage(sel, mode)`; absent = edit. The pencil stays as the second door, for the whole clause.
- **SEVERAL SUB-PARAGRAPHS ARE ONE PASSAGE** (reverses N-4's one-block wall): `ceSelectionRead` reads the range through `_negoNodeText` (breaks kept), finds the run of lines (a middle line ENDS with its part; the last part within a marker's width of its line, because the draft carries list numbers as text where the box draws them as styling) and carries `{line, lineEnd, multi, text}` in the DRAFT'S OWN FORM; `ceReplacePassage` / `ceCutPassage` splice the block; `ceMarkHeld` wraps one span per text piece and puts the selection back over them; `ceWordsPattern` is the one finder (whitespace-blind, a marker token optional). `ce_sel_two_paras` is inert. On the paper the reading was already fine — the silence was a drag RELEASED ON THE PENCIL (`selOutside`: a selection standing outside the control the mouse-up landed on still counts).
- **THE PIN**: the switch says `ng_np_tab_int` / `ng_np_tab_ext`, Internal lit at rest; no caption on a highlight pin (`ng_np_pin_on`, `ng_np_for_team`, `ng_np_for_them` STALE); a filed pin keeps its one line. **ADD NOTE SPENDS THE PIN**, and a draft in the other room is posted to ITS room in the same breath (`rlNotesSend`'s `second`, handed `extra.pin`). A quote keeps its breaks: `negoNoteQuote` is the one shape (pin, anchor); `rlWrapWords`, `ceAttachWords`, `ceRangeForText` join words with `\s*`; the Word range goes on the quote's longest line.
- **THE PRESS THAT OPENS A SLIDING PANEL CLOSES IT** (the owner's rule): the marker (`_rlNpOpenKey` + `notesPanelShowing`, published from js/app.js), the card's Notes row (no `force`), their page's door and marker (`_ptNotesKey`). `force` is for a filing that DELIVERS a pin, never for a door a person presses.
- **THE MARKER'S NUMBER IS INSIDE ITS DISC**: `.rl-clause .rl-note-mk` is longhands with fallbacks — the `font` shorthand was thrown away whole in the editor (no `--n-font-ui` above it) and the number inherited the paper's 15px/1.75. **CHANGED MEANS CHANGED**: the live clause draws `is-changed` only where `live.moved` (the editor passes `_ceText !== _ceBase || _ceHead !== _ceHeadBase`) or a filed change sits on it.
Tests: f304, round-two-comments-verify; f245, f303, F96, clause-door-verify 16d4, clause-editor-verify 18a0 re-pointed to the three verbs.

## COMMENTS, ROUND THREE — EVERY HIGHLIGHT OFFERS, THE PIN IS ONE SHAPE, THE NEGOTIATE PAGE BLURS (Young, 11 Sep 2026, late)

Eight reports off round two's first use (WORKORDER-comments-round-three.md), built on "go". **THE PRINCIPLE**: highlighting is THE way in — wherever contract wording is highlighted, the verbs are offered, no pencil first; the pencil is for typing a clause yourself. Silence on a highlight is a fault.
- **ONE READING FOR BOTH PAPERS**: `rlPaperOfferFromRange(ctx)` (js/views/negotiation.js) decides what a highlight is offered — one clause's OWN words (the whole highlight is that clause's share; a heading-only spill into the next clause is dropped first) → Ask · Edit · Comment through the editor's door with `passageMode`; across two clauses, a drag that begins in the recital, or the front matter → Ask (the Copilot panel, `rlAskCopilotPanel` → `docAiRead` — **since 23 Sep 2026 the front region is Ask · Edit · Comment and Ask across two clauses opens the EDITING Copilot on the first clause; see SEVEN FIXES 4**) and Comment (anchored to the first clause's share, or `negoFrontClause`) — **NEVER EDIT outside one clause** (the editor opens on one clause; merging numbered clauses renumbers an instrument). Their seat Comment alone. The negotiation paper's handler and the editor's `ceOfferOnPaper` (a range on `#ce-doc` outside `#ce-clausebody`; Ask/Edit on another clause → `ceGoClause` with the words in hand, on the same clause → `ceAttachWords`) both call it; the editor grows no second reading. F96 (B3)(B8)(B8b) re-pointed from silence to the offer.
- **THE FILED PIN IS THE HIGHLIGHT PIN'S SHAPE**: `rlNpPinHtml` draws reference · `<q>` · the switch for both; a filed pin quotes the change's wording (`rlNpChangeQuote`); no lead line (`ng_np_pin_filed`, `ng_np_pin_revised` STALE, `.rl-np-pin .lead` gone); Skip stays the way out. **TWO EQUAL HALVES**: `.rl-np-pinroom` is `inline-grid` with `grid-auto-columns:1fr`, measured equal whichever is lit.
- **THE NEGOTIATE PAGE BLURS BEHIND THE DRAWER** (reverses 27 Aug for this page only): `notesBlurs()` (js/app.js) = `state.view==='redline'` and the clause editor not open; `#panel-scrim.is-blur` is transparent with a 1.5px backdrop blur (the contract stays readable); the scrim opens with it, so pressing the blurred page closes the drawer (the alerts panel's rule). Every other page, the contract room and the clause editor keep no shade and no outside-press close.
- **APPLY ENDS TYPING**: a Copilot card's Apply calls `ceReplacePassage(card.passage, card.text, { keepView: false })` — `keepView` is the caller's (`o.keepView !== false`); the reader's own typing keeps it. **NO READING LIST UNDER A QUESTION**: `ceAsk` under the ask verb sends `read: []` and the answer card draws no OUR PLAYBOOK / WHAT WE SETTLED / THE WORDING rows; under Edit they stay.
Tests: f304 (8)(9), round-two-comments-verify H1–H5; f245, f302, f303, F96, clause-editor-verify 25c re-pointed.

## COMMENTS, ROUND FOUR — REPLY ON ANY NOTE, DELETE BESIDE DONE, THE MARKS AT ONCE, THE BLUR OFF (Young, 11 Sep 2026, late night)

Eight reports (WORKORDER-comments-round-four.md), built overnight on the owner's go; the open choices taken as the order proposed and said in the summary.
- **THE BLUR IS GONE** (the owner's reversal within the hour): `notesBlurs` and `#panel-scrim.is-blur` deleted; the 27 Aug ruling stands on every page — the notes face draws no scrim, no outside-press close.
- **THE MARKS FOLLOW THE RECORD IN THE SAME BREATH**: `rlRepaintNoteMarks(c, opts)` repaints every mounted canvas (every `.rl-doc`'s parent) at the post (`rlNotesSend`) and at every act (`rlNpWireActs`'s `again`). **A MARKER LANDS IN ITS NOTE'S ROOM**: the press reads the room off the note (`rlNpNoteByKey`, `rlNpSetRoom`) before the drawer paints.
- **SUGGEST DELETING A WHOLE LINE removes the line**; only a clause that would be emptied is refused (`ce_inline_cut_all`). Noticed, not fixed: cutting the START of a paragraph leaves a lowercase remainder that the text→rich lift re-flows into the paragraph above.
- **REPLY ON ANY NOTE**: every note draws Reply with the ROOT's key (`data-rl-np-reply`) and its own (`-under`); `_rlNpReplyUnder` says where the box draws; the answer joins the root, FLAT (Word's shape; the export untouched). Done stays on the root.
- **DELETE BESIDE DONE**: drawn where `negoNoteAuthoredBy(m)` (the author, delivered or not); greyed with the reason once delivered (`ng_note_sent`) or on a root with replies (`ng_np_delete_replies`); `negoDeleteNote(c, home, m)` takes a null home (the contract's own thread) and refuses a root with replies — the model is the wall; one `confirmDialog` naming the words. Admin not widened.
- **THE NOTE COMES AT SAVE** (the owner's option b): a Copilot card's Apply files nothing; the Save control's hover says the drawer opens on filing (`ce_save_opens_note`).
- **THE VERB PUTS THE CARET IN THE BOX** (reverses round two's "attaching never takes the caret"): `ceFocusAsk` at the end of `ceAttachPassage`; `rlNpFocusFreshPin` on the first paint under a fresh pin (`p.fresh`); `ceHeldPassage()` is what the rail and the tests read once the live selection has gone.
- **THE MORNING AFTER (12 Sep 2026, three reports)**: (a) the selection menu picks on MOUSEDOWN, so the mouse-up lands on the paper and the editor's deferred read tore down the passage the verb had just attached (the answer came back for the whole clause) — `ceStampPick()` / `CE_MENU_PICK_MS` (600) — stamped at the ONE attach and at all THREE pick doors (the editor's own menu, the paper's offer `ceOfferOnPaper`, and `rlOpenClauseEditor` with `opts.passage`, the negotiate page's highlight, which attaches a tick after the open): a mouse-up inside that window ends the press, not a drag; (b) a REPLY never asks the crossing question (`!reply` in `rlNotesSend`'s guard — the room is the thread's own); (c) a refused Delete is GREYED (`.rl-np-act-b:disabled`) — it was disabled with the reason on the hover and nothing dressed it, so it read as a dead button.
Tests: f304 (10)(11–13); round-two-comments-verify I1–I6, J1–J3 (J1 red at the parent); f264 (5), f302 (3), f303 (5), clause-editor-verify 18c/21d/21f/25c/25h, clause-door-verify 16d5, notes-two-rooms-verify 2 re-pointed.

## THE COPILOT AUDIT — HONEST NUMBERS, REACH, PARITY, THE BLIND SPOTS (owner's order, 11 Sep 2026; WORKORDER-copilot-audit.md)

Eight phases, each a commit. Copilot added kronor to euros (918M against a true 833M) and could not reach most of the book.
- **MONEY (1)**: `copilotMoneyOf(c)` — `currency`, `valueInHomeCurrency` (fxHome, null where no rate), `homeCurrency`, `valueRateMissing` — on `copilotDetail` and every `copilotList` row; the list carries `valueTotalInHomeCurrency` over the WHOLE filtered set and `valueLeftOut` (fxMissing). **THE WALL**: `COPILOT_MONEY_KEYS` / `copilotStripMoney`, and every new key on `AI_VALUE_FIELDS`; asserted off a restricted member both ways. `COPILOT_MONEY_NOTE` / `AI_MONEY_NOTE` on list_portfolio and get_contract, both hosts; the STABLE system block names the workspace currency and says each contract states its own. `aiPortfolioSnapshot`'s stream half converts and says what it left out. `_localMoneyOf` is the browser twin.
- **ARCHIVED (2)** off `copilotList` and the workspace line on both hosts (NOT_ARCH in the counts query); `archived:true` includes the shelf. **THE ID IS A COUNTER (3)**: one line in both stable blocks.
- **ONE PREDICATE, TWO HOSTS (4)**: `js/graphwhere.js` (`graphWhereNarrow`, `graphWhereHit(card, w, {daysUntil, folderIdOf})`; publishes no name a view declares) — the graph view's `graphWhereIds` and the server's `copilotList` both ask it, the server over `copilotCardOf(c, briefs)` (its own readings: `copilotObState`, `contractSignedOn`, the stored review, the briefs table, `copilotNegotiation`'s turn); `GRAPH_WHERE_SCHEMA` on the graph tool and on list_portfolio; the browser's list applies `graphWhereIds`. On js/app.js, test/world.js and test/dom.js's lists. f305 pins the key SET across hosts.
- **PAGING (5)**: `offset` → `offset, nextOffset, total, truncated`; the 40-row cap stays. **SEARCH (6)**: `copilotSearch(ctx, q, limit)` bounded 1–40 (`COPILOT_SEARCH_MAX`), FTS paged (`LIMIT ? OFFSET ?`) with scope on every page, `total` is the true in-scope count, `scanCapped` past `COPILOT_SEARCH_SCAN`; `AI_CHAT_TURNS` 14 (was 8 — a judgement call, flagged to the owner).
- **PARITY (7)**: the browser's `check_against_playbook` (stored review first, `playbookReviewHeuristic` where none, `noPlaybook` honest); the two tool-NAME sets pinned equal. **THE BLIND SPOTS (8)**: `get_obligations` (`copilotObligations` / the browser borrowing `allObligations`, `obState`, `obligationBand`, `obligationOwner`, `obligationAmount`, `obligationBlocked`; `byBand`, cap 60) and `get_contract_history` (`copilotHistory`; newest first, cap 60, money lines `redacted` for a no-values reader); folder scope, READING MUST NOT WRITE (pinned as a byte-identical record), never in PORTAL_MODE.
Tests: f305 (33 claims, eight sections); f218, f216, f151, f202, f203, f230, f299, f133, f232, f48, f290, insights-panels-verify.

## A NOTE ON ONE REDLINE, AND CHAT IS WHERE THEY ARE READ (owner-ruled 31 Aug – 2 Sep 2026)

A note tied to a redline is an ORDINARY MESSAGE on the change's own thread (no store, no route; `byId` and `sentAt` are new fields on a MESSAGE). **THE NOTE IS EXTERNAL BY DEFAULT — the explanation the other side reads** (reversed 1 Sep; since 11 Sep the window ASKS which room, External lit at rest — see THE NOTE WINDOW HAS TWO ROOMS). A — filed first, then asked (no press in the dialog can cost the redline; the window is a RECEIPT: a tick and "CHG-011 filed" as the headline, what was already said printed above the box, one row of acts; `#rl-note-chat`, `ng_note_others_*`, `ng_chat_lead` stale). B — the pencil FILES on the way out where `ceCanFile` says so (typing goes off only where the record moved; `ceFile` answers null on refusal). C — the change's own Notes row raises the same dialog with Save and Delete (one builder, two shapes read not passed). D — asked once (31 Aug) is REVERSED 11 Sep: asked on every filing; `revisions.length === 0` now only tells the window whether to say it is a revision. THE THREE ACTS (`negoMyNote` / `negoEditNote` / `negoDeleteNote`, beside `negoPostComment` the one writer): NOT YET DELIVERED (`negoNoteDelivered` reads `sentAt`, stamped only where `negoPostToChannel` came back ok), our side only, your own words only (id first, name second), reading `ch.thread` not the merged thread; every one writes an audit line.
- **THE DOOR IS IN THE CONTRACT'S ACTS ROW SINCE 20 SEP 2026** (was the shell bar). CHAT is the drawer's Notes face pressed with no change in hand (`openNotesPanel` with an optional change; `rlChatPanelHtml` draws every note on every live change plus the contract's own thread, oldest last, each row naming its change — the REFERENCE alone, `flex:none`, never wrapping; `ng_chat_on` stale); it wears the per-change panel's clothes (tabs, list; no `.rl-np-who`); no composer of its own except the contract-level one (below). **PRESSING CHAT AGAIN SHUTS IT** (10 Sep): the bell's rule with the scope (face + contract + change) in it; the old scope is read before the new one is stored; never cleared on the way out. ## THE ROW IS THE WHOLE OF IT — OPEN IS GONE (Young ruled 15 Sep 2026)

*"The artifact does not have the open button."* … *"Remove it."* **THIS REVERSES 2 Sep 2026's "one control on the face"**: the button and the body it unfolded are gone from our seat's row, and nothing behind it was lost.

Ours is Edit · Send · Discard, then the rest. `rvCancel` rides as `tail`; Ladder is last. **THE LOCKED DOOR IS STILL THE DOOR** (`rlLockedBtn` carries no data attribute — matched by `rl-verb-ai is-locked`, relabelled around its `<b>` monogram; missing it moved the shared verb column 27px → 128px, redline-verify 25m). The face wears BOTH `rl-card-face` and `rl-card-verbs`.

- **STALE**: `rlCardBodyHtml`, `rlCardBodyNotesHtml` (no caller on this seat), `.rl-card-open`, `.rl-open-cv`, `.rl-cb-wrap`; `_rlCardOpen` / `rlCardOpenId` / `rlCardSetOpen` are INERT (the suite's `test/cards.js` still sets and restores them). `.rl-open-btn` STAYS — it is the counterparty seat's own Open onto the clause panel.
- **THE COUNTERPARTY'S SEAT AND THE CONTRACT TAB ARE UNTOUCHED** and keep the receipt and full shapes, the body builder's dressing and the Copilot violet.

## THE MARK BESIDE THE WORD, AND THE STRIP IS A BAND (Young ruled 17 Sep 2026)

Off two renders. *"the shell's own hairline symbols beside each word … color code the edit button to be the some color as today and discard to be in red"*, and *"the card is always fixed at [providing] the header and only two lines below it."*

- **THE ROW'S VERBS**: `RL_FACE_MARKS` + `rlFaceMark` (js/views/negotiation.js, beside rlLockedBtn) — ONE table, ONE injection, applied to the whole face in `rlRowFaceVerbs` after the ordering, so a verb added tomorrow is dressed by the same rule and the row cannot end up half marked. **KEYED ON THE DOOR**, never the class (six verbs wear `.rl-edit`) or the words (translated). **NO COLOUR IS NAMED** — the symbol is stroked in `currentColor`, so each mark takes its verb's own ink; Edit keeps what it has and Discard stays `--st-ruby-fg`, both asserted as WALLS. **A LOCKED DOOR TAKES THE MARK TOO**: leaving it off draws it 21px narrower than the free door beside it, which is the column redline-verify 25m exists to hold still. Three new sprite symbols (`i-bin`, `i-ladder`, `i-undo`); the other six are reused by name. `.rl-verb-i` is 15px inside the verb's own 18px line, so the row does not grow; asked with `:has()` so an unmarked verb is left exactly as it is. **THE HARNESS PAGES CARRY NO SPRITE**, so `<use>` paints nothing there and only the box is measurable — the PAINT is measured on the real app.
- **THE ARRIVAL STRIP**: the tile body is **ALWAYS DRAWN** (`ktTriageStripHtml`) and the sheet holds exactly two lines (`height:calc(1.4em * 2)` beside its own `line-height`, plus a 2-line clamp). **RESERVING IS WHAT FIXES THE HEIGHT** — the tiles are a grid and a grid stretches every cell to the tallest, so a clamp alone would cap the long one and still let the strip shrink when nothing had anything to say. Measured on one record: 0, 1, 3, 4 and 10 lines, strip 224px → 123px, and the same height with a long detail as without. A cut is not a silent trim: the whole detail is on the hover (the obligations worklist's idiom) and the count in the chip already says how much there is. Home draws the same five readings under its OWN classes (`.hm-tri-*`) and is untouched; the pre-signature check borrows `.kt-tri` by id without borrowing the tiles.
Tests: f246 (12), f273 (10), redline-verify 29g/29g2/29j/29k (29g REVERSED in place), auto-triage-verify 14, type-and-symbols-verify 4.

## SETTINGS & RULES — FOUR TABS AND ONE DRAWER (13 Aug 2026 onward)

People · Platform settings · Build & launch · You; every row opens the same right-side drawer (✕ / scrim / Escape). ST_TABS, settingsTab()/settingsGoTab() per sitting; openSettingsAt(tab,panel) is the ONE named door (lands at the top); SET_PANELS is the registry (tab, mandatory, state(), body(), wire()). renderTeam() ITSELF is the gate — a non-admin is drawn renderMyAccountPage() (openMyAccount, the same builder tab 4 draws); SET_CLOSURES names what closed (f193 fails if empty). Two feet: 'save' and 'done' (gates write ON CHANGE and have no Save). A refusal sits in the drawer's FOOT (stDrawerRefuse / stDrawerClearRefusal). THE PAGE HOLDS STILL: patch in place, NEVER renderTeam() (settingsPaintShapeBoxes, settingsMarketFactsHtml; a refusal puts the screen back; the one full redraw is the market moving the LANGUAGE, with settingsHoldHeights flooring every panel).
- PEOPLE: one drawer for add and edit; stPersonMissing(u) is the ONE reading behind chip, banner and refusal; role list SAFEST FIRST; a rename can orphan an approval rule (said out loud, rules repointed); settingsWriteFolderAccess never sends `[]`. Signing cap (signCapOf(u) → {answered, limit}; three states; warn before enforce; an admin is never capped; isMonetary first; signCapBlocker joins signBlockers; server signCapOn / signCapOfRow; PATCH takes signCap as an admin grant; signCapLadder four bands). Who is checked is per person (see INTERNAL REVIEW). Which folders may this person sign in (signFolders.by, its own atomic route PUT /api/settings/sign-folders; only ever NARROWS; the switch rides the blob, the map does not; an empty array refused). Needs approval to sign (since 23 Sep 2026 a per-person rule — Off | Always, approver, backup, when; see A NAMED PERSON'S YES BEFORE ANYONE SIGNS; `overseerFor` has no caller, OVERSEER_STEP_ID still names the drawn step, nobody approves themselves). The lost-phone rescue button. f193–f197, f199, f202, settings-tabs-verify.
- BUILD & LAUNCH: stGoLive is derived from live facts; samples are cleared by ORIGIN (`seeded`) through POST /api/demo/clear; the integrity check is read-only. The outbox panel's two buttons go BUSY and answer 'ok'; stLoadOutbox answers {ok,n}. Settings & Rules has its own menu symbol (sliders).
- COMPANY & MARKET holds the legal identity (moved from the design step; PUT /api/org/branding — A KEY THIS SAVE DOES NOT CARRY IS A KEY IT DOES NOT TOUCH; identity fields admin-only as a difference; own Save; `passwordCurrent` is a first-login gate, not a per-save credential). f201. Folders: built-ins are literals; custom folders are per-browser and the panel says so.
- COPILOT ENGINE: the key, the money and what came of it are on screen; everything else (routing, by-person spend, limits, both switches, rates, backfill) is one `<details>` (`set_more_settings`, shut by default, remembers nothing). WHAT COPILOT COSTS PER PERSON: aiWho(req) is the one reading; every metered call passes `who:` (f203 walks every `await anthropicMessages(...)`); ai_spend_user beside ai_spend; `unattributed` is a figure. WHAT COPILOT PROPOSED (`js/aitrace.js`): `c.aiTrace`, five outcomes (proposed · as-is · edited · refused · read; `proposed` never counted as a refusal), settled at the funnel for OUR side, the hash for equality only, three recording surfaces (the editor's card at arrival, the playbook at the press — only `draft` is Copilot's, the obligations at the draw), Draft from a sentence deliberately not recorded, `AI_TRACE_MAX` 40; the evidence pack states what it is not (English); the panel prints COUNTS with shares in tiles (`aiTraceShares`, NOT TAKEN takes the residual), says when counting started, `aiTraceHistory` counts what the book already holds (never playbook filings). f203, f276, settings-tabs-verify.

### THE LIST IS GROUPED, SEARCHED, AND LED BY WHAT IS OWED (Young ruled 18 Sep 2026, off the "HaTi — Design Direction" canvas)

*"Implement the admin and settings as proposed but do not touch the corners."* MEASURED: 24 panels, **SEVENTEEN on one tab**, one height, one box, no search, no order — seventeen equal things read as none. `--radius` is UNTOUCHED; every new rule reads `var(--radius)`.
- **`ST_GROUPS` IS THE ONE ORDERED REGISTRY** (five groups on platform, two on build; keys STABLE ENGLISH, the order there IS the order on screen, panels keep SET_PANELS' order inside a group). Every panel carries a `group`; **`stGroupKeyOf` drops an ungrouped panel into its tab's LAST group** rather than off the page (f170's fault in this page's costume). `stPanelKeys(tab)` asks `show()` ONCE, so groups, search and attention cannot disagree about which panels exist.
- **THE TAG IS NARROWED, NOT DELETED**: Mandatory/Optional was a fact about the SETTING; the dot and the state line are facts about THIS WORKSPACE. `Required` prints only where `mandatory && dot!=='ok'`; `st_optional` is INERT in both books. `stLegendHtml` says what the three dots mean, once.
- **ONE BOX OVER ALL 24, EVERY TAB** (`stSearchHits`: name, sub, group, tab, state text — tags stripped first, that line carries markup). A cross-tab hit names its tab (`.st-row-where`). **`stPaintList` repaints `#st-list` ALONE** — never renderTeam, which empties seven server-filled panels and takes the caret out of the box; bound once per element (`dataset.stQBound`); a tab press clears the query.
- **THE ATTENTION BLOCK CROSSES TABS ON PURPOSE** (`stAttentionKeys`; `ST_ATTENTION_MAX` 4, then it counts): the panel that stops mail leaving the building lives on Build & launch. Drawn only when non-empty, every row a door. `stStateOf` catches a throw from a tab whose data has not landed. **`stRepaintRow` repaints EVERY instance** (a panel can sit in the block AND its group — querySelector answered the first and left the other stale) and `stSyncAttention` drops a settled row; rows only ever LEAVE, never arrive under the reader's hand.
- **THE DRAWER SAYS WHERE IT IS**: `stPanelPlace` gives the group, `n of total` and the next key; the crumb draws above the shared head (clear of the ✕) and the foot offers `Next: <name>` — **never on the one `foot:'save'` panel**, where it would silently discard typed edits. Additive: the person and account drawers pass no place and are byte-identical.
- NOT TAKEN, said out loud: the drawer BODY is not on the second surface. `.st-sec` carries three interlocking rules with documented reasons and only 12 of 24 bodies use it.
Tests: settings-groups-verify (33 checks, **30 red at the parent**; 2c, 5d and the page-error sweep are NAMED CONTROLS); f193's "the tab a row sits on" re-pointed in place (a flat-order compare became the tab's own groups, plus a claim for the block); f148 SAME_IN_BOTH gained `st_grp_copilot`.

## THE REMOVE KEY VERB SAYS WHAT IT CAN DO (Young reported it 21 Sep 2026)

*"I can save but i cannot delete"*, of the Copilot key, after moving it onto the server's own environment. **MEASURED against a real server before a line moved: the press was never broken** — it clears the key stored in the workspace and the read afterwards answers `{configured:true, source:'env'}`, which is right. What was missing is that the SCREEN said nothing: the status line reads "Configured" before and after, so the only visible difference is four masked characters and a parenthetical, and once the key comes from the environment the press is a NO-OP that still shows a confirm and a toast. A dead button wearing a live one's clothes — this file's own named fault class.
- **THE APP CANNOT DELETE THE SERVER'S OWN KEY AND MAY NOT PRETEND TO.** `aiKey()` is settings-first, env-second; the env half is removed where the environment is edited (on Render, the service's Environment settings), and the button says so.
- **ONE READING, ONE PAINTER, TWO HOMES**: `stKeyRemovable(source)` → `{can, why}` and `stPaintKeyClear(source)` (js/views/settings.js, both published), asked at every paint by the local-mode panel AND the server one — `stEngineBodyHtml` draws that control twice and a rule written twice drifts. Both buttons ship `disabled` in the markup, because until the read lands nobody knows whether there is anything to remove. **`undefined` IS "NOT KNOWN YET" AND IS NOT "THERE IS NOTHING TO REMOVE"**: a failed read must not claim an absence, so the catch path paints it rather than leaving it live.
- **THE CONFIRM NAMES THE REAL COST**, which needed one honest boolean: `GET /api/ai/config` gains `envKey` (a fact, never the key — `source` already reveals it whenever nothing is stored). With a server key waiting underneath, removing the stored one does NOT fall back to the built-in interpreter and the dialog no longer says it does. Per sitting, never persisted.
- The greying is written ON the element (inline styles on that button would beat a sheet rule), opacity .5, cursor default, the reason on `title` and `aria-disabled`.
Tests: f350 (22 claims, 11 red at the parent; four are NAMED CONTROLS that pass on both sides and are the measurement proving the press itself works), settings-tabs-verify (4 checks, **3 red at the parent**, which prints `disabled:false · title:"" · opacity 1 · cursor pointer` under a line reading *(from server env)* — the owner's report reproduced; the fourth is the CONTROL asserting the greying BOTH WAYS).

## DOES MONEY PASS UNDER THIS CONTRACT

isMonetary(c) is the ONE answer: the RECORD wins where it has said something (the "none" tick-box), the TEMPLATE answers silence (TEMPLATES.ND carries valueType:'none'), an upload is assumed to carry money. _repairValueType repairs narrowly (template says no money AND no value ever entered). readinessPanelHtml counts what BLOCKS. nda-carries-no-money-verify. **A VALUE LEFT EMPTY IS NEVER STAMPED 'none'** (23 Sep 2026 — see SIGNING WITHOUT THE FACTS): a company standard answers from its `nda` category, everything else writes nothing.

## SIGN LINKS AND SIGNERS

- A REVIEW LINK CANNOT SIGN: POST /api/shares/:token/respond refuses action:'sign' on an explicit purpose 'negotiate' (403). A Sign link binds to the stored route (shareSignerPickHtml; internal rows drawn and not pickable). "Propose a different value" is gone from the signing panel; the reading side stays.
- WHO THE LINK IS ADDRESSED TO: shareModalPrefill (js/core.js) — shareRouteRecipient (the counterparty signer whose TURN it is), then the last link, then Key terms — returns `source`; three doors prefill (dialog, phone sheet, route editor); counterpartyContact does NOT read the route. f182, share-recipient-verify.
- NAMING THE SIGNERS IS WHAT OPENS SIGNING: signingRouteOpen(c) (a named signer on EACH side) / signingRouteMissing(c); FOUR doors ask (doSend, POST /api/shares, POST …/respond — the wall — and wsNextAction); executed contracts exempt at mint. ONCE ANYONE HAS SIGNED THE ROUTE IS SHUT (signingLocked reads BOTH stores; openSigningLockedNotice replaces the editor; signingRestart MINTS FRESH ROW IDS; the server permits only the full restart, recognised by its result). defaultSharePurpose answers 'negotiate' while nobody is named; 'view' is offered on both shells. saveSignerPlan is the ONE authority on naming signers (both editors). Every test issuing a signing link needs nameASigner(client,id). sign-links-verify.
- AN INTERNAL SIGNER IS TOLD WHEN IT IS THEIR TURN: notifyInternalSignerTurn (server) is the ONE door (PUT when the turn MOVED, releaseNextSignerLink, the resend route); an app URL (contractSignUrl → `#contract=<id>&tab=sign`, honoured by openFromHash in startApp), never a token; /api/contracts/:id/notify-signer takes signerId only and refuses a body address; signer_notices rides back; signerNoticeState (js/approvals.js) draws the badge and resend (data-sp-notify). f185, f136.
- SIGNING ASSURANCE: see PHASE 3. THE ROUTE SHOWS ONCE SIGNED: see also A SIGNING TAB / SIGNING ON THE PAPER.

## THE OVERVIEW — ONE PAGE OF NAMED SECTIONS (owner-approved 16 Sep 2026, "HaTi's Next Fifteen" screen 3)

`js/section.js` is the grammar and is a pure builder — no route, no store, no field. FIVE RULES: name the group; **a shut group still answers** (the head carries a summary); open what is acted on, reference opens shut; label above value with an EM-DASH for silence (`sectionFieldHtml` writes it); a row of controls becomes one sentence (`sectionStatementHtml`). The fold is PER SITTING AND IN MEMORY — a stored fold would make two people's screens disagree. `sectionWire(root, repaint)` binds once per element and **reverses what `aria-expanded` says**, never a guessed default. A summary is drawn only while a FOLDABLE section is shut; a head that cannot be opened always draws it and wraps rather than elides. `.sec-*` lives in index.html and is the only dressing.
**A SECOND SCREEN TAKES THIS GRAMMAR ONLY ON THE OWNER'S WORD.** Home, Contracts, Insights and Requests were restyled with it on 16 Sep and **REVERTED the same day** — the owner asked for the fifteen, and that artifact promises those four pages "weight added: none" / "nothing else moves". **THE WORD CAME ONCE, 19 Sep 2026**, for the Templates page's book tab (see THE TEMPLATES PAGE IS TWO TABS): the owner approved "The Template Book" artifact and said build it. What went with it is the RULE SET, not the look — named groups, a shut group that still answers, open what is acted on — and the fold repaints that page and nothing else. Everywhere else the rule stands unchanged.
### THE RECORD AND THE DEAL ARE THE ARTIFACT'S GRID, NOT THE OLD ROWS (Young ruled 17 Sep 2026)

*"this is how the record card is designed in the artifact but this is not what you have built. I never sanctioned what you have built in image 2. Review what else I did not sanction and fix it."* The first build pasted the OLD Key terms rows (label left, editable box right) inside the new named sections. The artifact draws a four-column label-above-value grid.
- **BOTH SECTIONS REST AS A GRID AND CARRY NO EDITABLE BOX.** The deal is the artifact's twelve (value, payment terms, rebate, tiers, price review, rejection window, liability cap, exclusivity, effective, expiry, notice, governing law) plus term, renewal and category — the artifact counts TWENTY terms shown, so more is the design. The record is its twelve filing attributes (reference, our party, counterparty, their email, value stream, template, owner, status, raised, signed, filed by, last updated).
- **`ktFactReads(c)` IS THE ONE READING** both shapes ask, and it returns the RAW value: the grid's silence is `sectionFieldHtml`'s em-dash and the row's is "Not set", and each shape is entitled to its own. What may not differ is the value when there is one.
- **NOTHING THAT EXISTS WAS REMOVED** (the artifact's own promise): every editable row is behind **`Edit these details`**, per section, per contract, in memory (`_ovEdit` / `ovEditing` / `ovSetEditing`). A grid whose rows are drawn gives those fields up — `opts.rowsAbove` plus a skip set keyed by NAME — so no fact is printed twice. The deal gets the act though the artifact names none there: a grid with no way in would take away the only place the value and the dates can be answered.
- **`Move to another stream` IS NOT A SECOND DOOR**: it opens the rows and presses the stream row a reader presses; the picker, its admin guard, its 'Re-filed' line and its repaint stay `ktStreamRowHtml`'s. **IT SURVIVES SIGNING** (`mayMove` is admin, not `ed`) — filing is housekeeping and D-1 says so; refile-a-contract-verify caught the hour it did not.
- **TWO ADDRESSES MAY DIFFER, NEVER QUIETLY**: `ktRouteEmailRead` is the reading, the disagreement is a CELL on the resting card (amber) and the row behind the act, and `cpRouteEmail` is in the skip set so it is said once.
- **WHAT COPILOT READ IS THE ARTIFACT'S TABLE OF FIVE** (`ktReadingsRowsHtml`): brief, playbook pass, obligations, risk scan, plain English — each with what it found, the date, and a door (`data-ov-read-go` to `roomGoTab`). It BORROWS every figure and spends nothing; the brief's own card still draws below it, carrying the old Write the brief button. `docReadHeld` is asked BARE — it is declared in this file and f232 caught the `window.` guard that was always false.
- **RELATED AGREEMENTS GAINED ITS ORDER AND ITS CHECK (S12)**: `familyOrder` (parent, then executed children oldest first) and `familyCheck` (every child through `familyAgreement` — the rows' OWN reading — plus which document holds each moved term; an unsigned child is named and takes nothing). `familyPrecedenceLineHtml` states the rule; `Check the family` (`#fam-check`) reports it in one dialog. No route, no model, no write.

- **THE TAB IS `tab_overview`** — the KEY stays `'terms'` (every route, deep link and stored state is built on it) and `tab_key_terms` stays LIVE because the PHONE draws its own tab, deliberately untouched. One stack `.ov-stack`: the renewal card leads unwrapped (it names itself), then The deal (open) and The record (shut) — both rows from `ktTermsRowsHtml(c,{only:[…]})`, ONE builder, caller's order — then Documents they must hold, Related agreements and What Copilot read, the last two hosting `#family-section` / `#brief-card` drawn `bare` so the name is said once.

## THE CONTRACT ROOM — five tabs, one shell that draws them

Key terms / Document / Signing / Obligations / History from roomTabsHtml() via roomGoTab(); add tabs in ROOM_TABS (a list read twice, count as a third element); NEVER a second tab row. 'redline' still ROUTES through roomGoTab and names no tab. The room's back arrow goes to the CONTRACTS page ALWAYS (setView('register') constant; a stream drawer is the one survivor; the workbench's data-back="contract" lands on the Document tab). The head is built once per render: anything that changes underneath needs a SLOT and a paint from applyWsTabs (#ws-tabrow-end → wsPaintTabRowEnd; #ws-round-needs-slot → wsPaintRoundNeeds; #kt-triage → paintKtTriage) — wire where you PAINT, never in wireRoomHead/wireWsTabs. THE DOCUMENT TAB IS A CLEAN READ (docFillable(c): a DRAFT keeps editable blanks; from Under Review onward readOnlyDocHtml). HIGHLIGHT ON THE DOCUMENT TAB → SIMPLIFY / ASK COPILOT (DOC_SEL_ACTIONS via rlSelMenu's ctx.actions/onPick; reading aids, never edits — f211 greps for the funnel; never PORTAL_MODE; the full passage reaches the model). The status strip (`actionBarHtml`) is a stub on EVERY tab; `#ws-actionbar` hides when empty (`data-ws-fold` / `data-ws-display` stale). Key terms cards fill the page measure. A NEW DRAFT OPENS ON KEY TERMS (roomOpenOnTerms(id) / wsTabDefaults: ONCE; NOT-YET-EXECUTED only — uploads included; SEVEN creation sites register it and f170 fails on an eighth; js/family.js is the exempt eighth). A CONTRACT KNOWS WHOSE IT IS (`c.owner={id,name}`, contractOwnerStamp at all seven sites, stamped once; `_repairOwner` narrow; read through contractOwnerName/contractOwnedBy — stored owner first, then `_raisedBy` transport; auditDatesOf carries `_raisedAt/_signedAt/_lastAuditAt` for Reports). WHEN A CONTRACT WAS SIGNED: `contractSignedAt(c)` (js/negotiation.js, beside negoExecuted) is the ONE reading — a recorded day, the execution stamp in the signer's own clock (`execution.tzOffsetMin` / `tzLabel` written at signing; `pdfSigTime` keeps the old parse for older records), a legacy display string read by its words, the trail; ISO day or null; `contractSignedLabel(c)` is what a screen prints; `sealWhen(c)` is the server's one builder. Never write a backfill. Tests: f91, f148, f170, f198, f199, f211, f258, room-order-and-notices-verify, document-ask-copilot-verify.

One card, the design's row (time column, dot, event over actor, round at the right); the Versions card is retired (`roomVersionsHtml` has no caller; `.hist-grid`, `.hist-rail` stale); `histTone(e)` prefers the OUTCOME; filters stay attached, five in the open with a 132px floor and no ceiling, dressed like the Contracts page's; the head says which way the list runs with the count in the sentence. The Everyone/Ours/Theirs chips and the Filter lid are gone (`.hist-segs`, `data-ht-side`, `ct_whose_changes`, `ct_filter` stale); "⋯ More ▾" is a MENU (Verify / Export / Print), armed ONCE on document; Side reads from the reader's chair (negoTimelineSeatIsTheirs(opts) — explicit seat, else PORTAL_MODE; values never move). ### A LINE OF TIME, NEWEST FIRST (Young ruled 24 Sep 2026, over the "HaTi — Production Polish" canvas)

*"The way the history is chronicled, the line attaching the history and rounds. Also the sentences above the names should be in black font."*
- **NEWEST FIRST ON THE TAB ONLY**: `roomHistoryHtml` reverses; `roomHistoryEvents` (the reading) stays oldest first, and so do the pop-out record (`negoTimelineScreenHtml` — their page and the history link) and the export. `ct_hist_reading` says "Newest first" in both books. Every row carries `data-hist-at`.
- **THE DAY OVER THE TIME**: `histWhen(at, now)` (published) → `{day, time}` — `ct_hist_today`, else day + month in `langLocale()`, the year only where it is not this year; the time is `negoWhen`. A bare day is read as the LOCAL day and gets no time (never an invented 00:00); a non-date prints as it came. REVERSES 24 Aug's "the DATE ONLY" — every writer stamps a full time.
- **A RING AND ONE LINE**: the row is a grid inside ONE `.hist-trail`; the dot is a 13px ring (tone on the EDGE through `histTone`, the middle `--color-surface`, `z-index:1`); `.hist-ev::before` is the line in `--rule`, its left and both ends computed from the SAME trail variables as the grid and the ring (`--hist-when-w`, `--hist-gap`, `--hist-dot`, `--hist-dot-mid`); it starts and stops at a ring, one entry draws none. The rules between rows are GONE. REVERSES 24 Aug's "the rail's vertical line went" — the line came back with the ring it was drawn for.
- **THE ROUND IS `R1`** (`ct_round_short`, SAME_IN_BOTH) with `ct_round_n` on the hover; no round draws `—` (`.is-none`, aria-hidden), so the column always holds. **THE SENTENCE** (`.hist-text`) is `--color-text` at `--w-label`; the names line is untouched.
Tests: f380 (20 claims, 16 red at the parent), history-timeline-verify (33 checks in a real browser, 26 red at the parent).

- THREE READINGS AND A MORE: the switch is the one builder `rlReadSegsHtml` in their `.pw-id` header (row 2 since 23 Aug); `rlRepaintFrom` falls back to the page's one `.rl-embed`; `rlPaintReadSegs` paints faces; More is a MENU (PDF, Word, Focus mode; six rows deliberately absent); both exporters refuse to WRITE in PORTAL_MODE; exportWordTracked takes a `side`; `rlFocusPage` answers for whichever page is mounted; `body.pw-focused` stands their chrome down and the wall line survives; the header wraps on content.

Not on the phone. THE DELIVERY STATUS lives IN THE BELL (green arrived, grey in flight, silent on an older link — `portalDeliveryState` three readings; `applied` is the exact fact; `.pw-delivery` / `portalDeliveryLine` stale). `portalReadySpent()` has three readings in order (this sitting, the record, the server) and `applied` without readiness means REFUSED (the button comes back). The two reading buttons are drawn only where `portalHasHistory` / `portalHasCompare` answer yes.

- THE DEAL VERBS ARE A GROUP IN THE HEADER ROW: `#pt-nego-foot` (portalNegoFootHtml, `.pw-foot`, NEVER hidden — f180 walks ancestors); Send is on the held-decision card too (a proxy onto `#nego-send-decisions`); PORTAL_FOOT_COMPACT stands the strip's sentences down (every one findable elsewhere); the name box is gone (portalResponderName chain; portalEnsureResponderName asks ONCE); the adviser copy is handed over once (openDerivedLinkDialog; PORTAL_DERIVED etc. deleted); Ready to sign is in the header EXACTLY ONCE and is the real `#pt-nego-ready` (the mirror `#pt-ready-top` and focus mode of 12 Aug gone; `.ui-btn[hidden]{display:none!important}` in index.html); "Review what changed" on a signing link opens the RECORD (openPortalHistory), never a second workbench.

A model function may not draw: `negoSignalReady` returns null and the caller decides; a refused readiness claim is marked HANDLED (`return true`) so it stops arriving (four duplicate audit lines were the tell). Thirteen other model toasts are reported, not swept.
- "COUNTERPARTY READY TO SIGN" IS SAID WHERE THE READER IS LOOKING: `cpReadyToSign(c)` (js/core.js, through window in a try) is the ONE predicate for the head's status word (`READY_META`), the register chip (`READY_META_SHORT`) and the alerts row (`cp-ready`, green, FIRST); an overlay over `STATUS_META`, never a fourth status; `stale` comes free; the signal is never erased. GREEN IS NEWS: `rlBellIsNews(c)` / `rlReadySeen(c)` keyed on the signal's TIMESTAMP (`hati.v1.cpReadySeen`), marked seen in `renderContextPanel` after the paint; three blinks; `al-good` (tone) and `al-news` (seen) are two classes; the flash is the background.

## THE NEW DESIGN (Document + Negotiate, 10 Aug 2026) — what still governs

ONE SHEET FROM TOKENS on four screens (`--color-doc-warm` / `--color-doc-warm-line` / `--shadow-paper`; print pins white; the phone loads redlineLayoutCss()). TWO BUILDERS every document body goes through: docPaperHeadHtml and rlPaperFootHtml (drawn ONCE by signatureBlock — the builder that went unexported for a year). READ-ONLY COPY: renderShareViewer must NOT read c.redlineText — the owner's side renders `viewBody` ONCE at link-mint; a copy with neither says so. THE NEGOTIATION PAGE IS TWO FILES: js/views/negotiation.js (~11,700 lines) and js/views/negotiation-css.js (negoStyleHtml, redlineLayoutCss — an interface of two names, both published, listed first everywhere: js/app.js, test/world.js NEGOTIATION_VIEW array, test/portalworld.js, the four harness pages). Do not split further without the same measurement. THE TWO DRAWERS: docBody is one dispatcher (isUpload → uploadDocBody, executed → frozenDocBody, stored wording → redlineDocBody, else template); the two card renderers are correctly factored; `negoLeadChange(c, cl, chs)` is the ONE reading of which change a clause's paper draws (f210 (10) asserts one reading, two callers). WHERE A COLOUR LIVES: `:root` in index.html (120 tokens); negotiation-css.js reads it; never rewrite the second argument of a `var()`; deliberate literals: aichart.js, healthreport.js, weekly.js, `--n-*` (`--n-paper` stays white), JS colour tables. The share dialog arrives ONCE (the first paint is the real first step; shareWireOpening aborted before the fill). AN UPLOADED CONTRACT IS A CONTRACT, NOT A FILE ATTACHMENT: the wording is the page (no box; PDFs keep their preview — now the stored wording, see A PDF KEEPS ITS STRUCTURE); the file is ONE strip; the reader's text size reaches it (`scaled()`); the WORKING TEXT card lid is gone (`ct_working_text` stale). THE TERM IS ONE FACT: docTermSpan(c) both directions (a FILL, never an overwrite; DOC_TERM_IN_CLAUSE four of twelve); TEMPLATE_PAY per template; builtinTemplateFields copies by descriptor. PLAYBOOK FINDINGS ARRIVE OPEN (pbUI records what is SHUT; pbFoldKey; quotes in the panel only). Tests: readonly-copy-verify, term-and-fields-verify, playbook-opens-read-verify, f48, f210, f225.

## WHAT COUNTS AS A CLAUSE (js/clausemodel.js)

clauseSegment() is the ONE splitter. Two readings decided by HEADINGS: headings mark the clauses, or they don't (one clause per top-level block, title is front matter); clauseSegment, clauseFrontMatter and clauseStampIds share _clTitleIndex / _clHeadingsMarkClauses. `CLAUSE_KINDS` is the one vocabulary (PRECEDENT_TOPICS is built FROM it); `clauseKind` reads THE HEADING AND NOTHING ELSE (right on headed paper, BLANK on a wall of paragraphs; both `re` and `head` cues); a rule only touches a clause of its own kind in `rlPbFindClause` (containment is certainty about ONE clause — several are broken by kind, a tie is null; the overlap score is narrowed by kind, an UNKNOWN kind always stays a candidate — f131's WALL). ID DURABILITY: negoStampContract writes ids only into rich stored bodies; clauseCarryIds(prevHtml, nextHtml) makes a re-read return the SAME document. applyNegoProposals never drops what it cannot place (recover by wording, then label; unplaceable wording goes to the audit trail and reports HANDLED; a refused DECISION stays unhandled). A verb that cannot work is not drawn. Page BANNERS over numbered clauses ride with the clause after them (`_clBanners`; see SEVEN FIXES 2). Tests: f163, f131, f222, f371.

## TWO LANGUAGES ≠ TWO MARKETS

LANGUAGE is the PERSON's (users.lang, js/i18n.js); MARKET is the COMPANY's (js/jurisdiction.js, admin-only). A MONTH FOLLOWS THE LANGUAGE: langLocale() carries both halves ('en-SE'), memoised on (language, region); NUMBERS stay on jxLocale; THE CONTRACT never passes through either (fmtDocDate, DOC_MONTHS); f148 sweeps for a new screen printing a month through jxLocale. CONTRACT TEXT IS NEVER TRANSLATED. i18t() / i18tn(), NEVER t(). Traps: a dictionary call in a template literal is literal text; never inside a regex; never branch on translated words; a RECORD label keeps English; getters not literals; index.html is plain HTML. A key removed from one dictionary and not the other leaves a screen half-English — retire keys by leaving them inert in BOTH. Language toggle in the shell bar (into the nav drawer below 900px); market on Settings. `node test/chromium/lang-coverage.js` is a measure, not a test.

## THE CHARTS, AND THE HEALTH REPORT

ONE box of recipes: js/aichart.js (Copilot charts, the Intelligence dock, Reports' four cards with CSS fallback strips, the health report's PNGs). THE LIBRARY IS OURS TO SERVE: Chart.js in `vendor/` (NOT `js/`), served at `/vendor`, `AI_CHART_SRC`; the failure card no longer blames the reader's network (`hr_charts_offline`); analytics-verify aborts every CDN request (the OCR path still fetches from cdnjs — logged). The AI names a KIND and NEVER supplies data. A NAMED SHAPE IS BINDING: aiAskedShape(ask) / aiHonourShape(spec, ask) in aiExtractCharts; AC_SHAPE_SWAP holds only the kinds a breakdown reproduces EXACTLY; "bar" and "line" need chart context. Only two surfaces let the model pick a kind and neither holds a kind list. A chart you CLICK is inline SVG (friction bars, the risk map). The Portfolio Health Report is DETERMINISTIC (openHealthReport opens the tab synchronously, light palette, hati.v1.monthlySnaps in localStorage). The Copilot brief travels as ctx.guideRules + ctx.guideLive (two system blocks, cache_control on the first); error bubbles are excluded from aiChatMessages; f151 is the drift test. Tests: f177, f151, analytics-verify, insights-panels-verify.

`negoAddNamedClause` is the ONE act "add a clause under a name": it asks `negoDupClauseStop` (a clause already on the table as a pending ask, or already IN the agreement — exact after folding case and punctuation; withdrawn/superseded/rejected do not count, an accepted insert does; a `modify` is never a duplicate; reads `c.negotiation` only where one already exists — READING MUST NOT WRITE) and REFUSES (owner: "I want it to be impossible"), handing the refusal back on `opts.refused` and returning null; otherwise it presses `negoInsertClause`. `clauseHeadingFor` is the one heading builder, so the sign and the wall agree. THREE DOORS arrive at it (the Playbook review window, the clause editor's scan rail, the room's `#nego-insert-lib`); the Playbook side PANEL is a READING now (`applyClauseRedline` kept exported with NO caller; its list `pbProposedClauses(c)` reads the negotiation's pending `insertClause` asks raw; `pb_apply_suggested` stale). **The wall is NOT on `negoInsertClause`** — `negoFileProposal` and the editor's `fileAll` insert clauses the other side really added, and a wall there would drop their wording silently (f279 (10) pins the absence). Every sign (`Already here`) is drawn where the press can be known dead, with the way forward in words (`negoReviseInsert`, or withdraw); no sign writes. `pbShowInsert(c, x)` is the one "where did it go" walk, on the panel's proposed list only (neither surviving door walks you to the clause, deliberately). Tests: f279, playbook-opens-read-verify.

## THE READER'S TEXT SIZE — `--doc-scale`

The A⁻/A⁺ stepper stores a px (base 15, bounds RL_TYPE_MIN 8 to 20) and writes `--doc-scale` (rlDocScale, the RATIO) on the `.redline-page` roots, `#doc-zoom` and `#ds-zoom`; `.doc-surface`, `.hati-doc` and the paper rules read it through `calc(px * var(--doc-scale,1))`. Nothing else defines it, so print, exports and the portal take 1. THE PAPER SCALES, THE FURNITURE DOES NOT: the clause panel, the change cards, the retired plan band pin `--doc-scale:1`; marks ON the paper (ask tags, signature spots, the front matter, the foot) are paper and scale; POSITIONING stays in bare pixels. The magnification of the sheet to its column (`rlApplyDocZoom`) is history — pinned at 1. Tests: paper-grows-verify, f225.

**The book** answers WHAT HAVE WE GOT and **Templates** is the table. `TPL_PAGE_TABS` is the one list (`['book','list']`) and **the landing is `TPL_PAGE_TABS[0]`, which is the book** (Young ruled it 20 Sep 2026 — see THE LANDING IS THE FIRST TAB below; 18 Sep's "still the LIST" is REVERSED). **THE OVERVIEW TAB WENT THE DAY THE BOOK ARRIVED** (Young: *"delete the templates overview page"*, option (a) of three): its headline is the first line of the book's own glance, and two tabs leading with one number is the fault this product keeps paying for. THE BOOK (`tplBookHtml` / `tplBookRepaint`, Young ruled it 19 Sep off "The Template Book" artifact) is the old card wall in **HaTi's own section grammar** — `js/section.js`, which CLAUDE.md pins to the contract room's first tab "and nothing else" and releases only on the owner's word; **this is that word, and the Templates page is the second screen**. A glance of three figures leads (label over value, the caveat UNDER the rate it qualifies — never a band); three named sections follow, *Your library* open, *By value stream* SHUT so its head has to answer (`lib_bk_streams_sum` names the worst stream, and only where `scanned >= TPL_DEV_MIN` — a headline over a sample of one is not a headline); a fold repaints the BOOK, never the page (`tplBookRepaint`, the section element survives or `sectionWire`'s one listener goes with it). **ONE CARD BUILDER, TWO SCREENS**: `tplOvCardHtml` / `tplOvPanelsHtml` / `tplOvRateInk` were lifted out of `tplOverviewHtml` so the wall and the book cannot disagree — THE CLOTHES FOLLOW THE BUILDER. ## PROMPT & BUILD — THE TEMPLATE BUILDER IS THE PAPER AND THE RAIL (owner's go, 13 Sep 2026; round two overnight 13–14 Sep)

Round one put an ask box under every section; the owner called it clunky and asked for Copilot on the right "like in the contract workspace", where you TAG a section, TALK about it, and APPLY. `js/views/templatebuilder.js` draws the template as a document on the left (`tb-paperslot`, contenteditable blocks read back through `tbReadEditable` — chips become `{{key}}`) and Copilot in the clause editor's clothes on the right (`tb-railslot`); the two are SLOTS, so a partial repaint never rebuilds the box with the caret in it.
- **TAG**: five doors — ✦ on a section, the ◀▶ arrows, @ in the ask box, Next, Draft this — and ONE reading of "the section in hand", `tbFocus` (`_tb.focus = ` appears exactly twice: there and at open). **TALK**: a thread per section (`tbSend`, `tbTurn`); the model is shown the other sections "for consistency only" (`o.others` in `copilotProposeTemplate`). **APPLY** (`tbAccept`) is the ONE writer: accepted wording lands on the block exactly as a keystroke does. `tbAddBlock` is still the only push into `_tb.blocks`; block content is written in exactly three places (f306 pins all of it).
- "Walk me through it" (`tbSetWalk`) is ON after an outline (`tbOutlineAdd` — the playbook's required categories are folded against the model's list and each other, so a heading is never added twice); Playbook and Blanks are TABS (`tbSetTab`); below `TB_RAIL_MIN` (1024) the rail is not drawn (`tbRailFits`) and the paper keeps the page. The inline ask boxes are RETIRED. Five decisions taken as proposed in the artifact: the paper replaces the block list; walk on by default; no rail below 1024; the two pop-ups stay two; Apply does not save.
- What round one built still governs underneath: `POST /api/ai/outline` (headings and intent, no wording, metered), `copilotPropose({template:true})`, `tbLibraryFor` / `tbStandardFor` / `tbPrecedentFor`, `pbRangeRead` shared with the playbook, `tbCoverage` (a deviation is recorded and never blocks), proposed blanks in `_tb.proposed`, every failure spoken on its section (`tbSay`).
Tests: f306 (58 claims), prompt-and-build-verify (27 checks; `HATI_SHOT_DIR` puts its screenshots where the runner may write), f103, f148 (`tb_copilot` is the same word in both books).

"What kind of template?" is two equal `.tn-tile`s; "New standard template" puts Category and Value stream on ONE row (`tplLibCatStreamRowHtml`), and `tplLibCategoryOptions` lists Other ONCE.
