### THE FOUR EVERYDAY PAGES, AND THE GREY THAT WAS TOO FAINT (Young ruled 21 Sep 2026)

*"The home page in HaTi, contracts, negotations and calendar need to look exactly like the artifact. Pay attention to the relationship between grey fonts and black fonts in the artifact as well and apply them accordingly. There is a lot of faint grey that makes reading a bit hard. As far as the home page, the the prepared for you card and the needs your attention card have the same width as the space utilized by the combination of the top 4 cards."*

- **THE TWO HOME CARDS TAKE THE TILES' OWN WIDTH, AND THE ARTIFACT ITSELF DOES NOT.** Its `.stack` is capped at 1100 while its KPI row fills the page — the exact mismatch the owner is pointing at — so HaTi's `hmCard` states no width and MEASURES the tiles' (1334 at left 80, both, checked on every render). `hmSec` takes a card flag and `.hm-card` is the shell; the row's left tone bar is the reference's `.hm-rdot` now; the **"My work" heading is gone** (`home_my_work` STALE, inert in both books — four tiles that name themselves need no name above them), and the fourth default tile is `active_value`, not `avgcycle`.
- **THE INK LADDER: HaTi IS ONE STEP DARKER THAN THE REFERENCE WHEREVER THE REFERENCE USES ink-3, AND STAYS THERE.** The reference's `--ink-3` is HaTi's `--color-neutral-400`, which this file has recorded as failing AA as text since 22 Aug. A column head, a row's sub-line, a card head's sub and a tile's detail are all FACTS and read at `--color-neutral-500` or 600. **The faintest step is for an ABSENCE or for FURNITURE** — measured, what is left on these four pages is the idle sort arrow, the em-dash meaning the agreement says nothing, and an adjacent month's day number. Nothing else. **The census is the instrument**: every element that paints its own visible text, with its colour, read off a real page — three of the four Home tile figures were drawing 26px in the faintest grey and no rule in the source looked wrong.
- **THE ROW IS 13px, WHICH IS THE OWNER'S OWN NUMBER.** WO-16 ruled 14 down to THIRTEEN and it was written as `--t-meta`, which the redesign's ladder later made 12 — so the row had gone one rung further than anybody ruled. `.reg-table` is `--t-body` and so are `.reg-mk`, the badge, `.ngl-w` and the four inline sizes in the row region that had been left behind. **The title is `--w-label` in the page's own ink over a 12px sub-line in the quiet ink**, which is the reference's `.tbl td .t{font-weight:500;color:var(--ink)}` and IS the relationship the owner asked for. **TWO `.reg-table th` RULES AT EQUAL WEIGHT IN TWO SHEETS** were folded into one — the winner was whichever was injected first.
- **THE NEGOTIATIONS HEAD IS A SUB-LINE, LIKE EVERY OTHER PAGE'S.** "2 live · 2 waiting on you" under the name, not a steel chip beside it; `.ngl-live` KEEPS its class (four tests reach it by name) and is dressed at `.ngl-head-table .ngl-live` — **by SCOPE, because `.page-facts` and `.ngl-live` both score (0,1,0)** and source order would have decided it. The second half BORROWS `negoBandCounts(negoGroupByMove(…))`, the band's own arithmetic, so head and band cannot disagree. The band count is IN its heading (`· 2`), no pill.
- **THE CALENDAR'S MONTH IS SAID ONCE.** The stepper (`calMonthStepHtml`, the same three ids) moved into the page sub-line, so the grid card begins with the weekday row exactly as the reference's does; `.cal-cardbar` stays for the Horizon. **The agenda window is a segment of four numerals** (`data-cal-days`, id `cal-days` KEPT) — this REVERSES "the heading IS the control" of 24 Aug, whose objection is still written beside the code: the owner has seen the drawing and a window changed four times a sitting is the wrong place to charge two presses. ONE VALUE still answers the heading, the lit half, the reading and the empty state.
- **NOT BUILT, and each would invent something**: the reference's KPI sparklines (a trend HaTi does not hold), a search box on the Contracts page (the shell bar's already narrows it — one door), its own quick-filter tab set, and its Round / Open asks / Last move / Owner columns on Negotiations. The value-stream cell elides because HaTi's stream names are four words where the reference's are one; the whole name is on the hover.
Tests: home-page-verify 53/53, white-band-and-tabs-verify 45/45, calendar-day 23/23, kpi-four 19/19, row-density, laptops, pages-read-alike, signed-and-columns. Re-pointed IN PLACE with the reason beside each: home-page 2/3, white-band-and-tabs 4a–4d and **5d/5e (the seventeen-property match against a RESTING TAB stopped being true when the redesign made a resting tab quiet — a tab is furniture, a row title is the row's identity; the ink is asked of the page's own heading now)**, calendar-redesign 1, **flat-rows 5b (the kind's exception was pinned by CLASS NAME, and the class moved) and 5c (reversed: at most TWO weights, the title carrying the heavier, nothing heavier than the column head)**, f184 (three claims — the count LEADS the line; the band claim pinned the punctuation with the digits), f261 (2).

### THE SHELL BAR WEARS THE WORKSPACE'S OWN COLOUR (Young ruled 21 Sep 2026)

*"The top highlighted bar, make it the color of the color mode the user chooses, Green on blue. Adjust the rest of the features to fit in without disappearing in the background."*

**THIS REVERSES HALF OF DECIDE 4** (20 Sep, "the dark 44px bar is gone"), and only the half about the BAR: the rail still runs floor to ceiling in white with the mark at its top, the bar is still 48px in the page column, and it still keeps every control it had.
- **THE GROUND IS `--nav-bg`, THE TOKEN IT WORE BEFORE THE REDESIGN** — #093733 green, #0F2648 navy, its own answer under dark — stated on the element, because the rest of this bar's geometry is an inline style and no stylesheet rule could have reached past it. Nothing here names a colour; the brand moves and the bar moves with it.
- **FIVE TOKENS ON THE BAR, READ BY EVERY RULE UNDER IT**, because "so they do not disappear" is a ladder and not a list: `--bar-ink` for a control that carries a word, `--bar-ink-2` for furniture, `--bar-line` for an edge, `--bar-well` for an inset, `--bar-hover`. **WHITE AT AN ALPHA over the ground**, so each brand and the dark theme compose their own. **THE FOCUS RING IS WHITE, NOT THE ACCENT** — the accent ramp IS this ground, so an accent ring on it is a ring nobody sees; the one rule that could not simply be re-tokened.
- **THE WELL IS 10%, AND THAT IS A MEASUREMENT.** At the bar's own earlier 14% the ⌘K chip (a well inside the search box's well) falls to 4.38:1; at 10% it is 5.55:1. Two stacked translucent layers are the thing to measure here.
- **EVERY CONTROL WAS RE-INKED** and each was read off a real page: the title, the crumb and its separator, the search box and its placeholder, the ⌘K chip, the language pair (its own class and its own accent fill, which on this ground would be the bar's colour on the bar), the two swatches and the lit ring (drawn in `--nav-bg`, so it reads as a gap), the theme word, Copilot, alerts, activity, the avatar, sign-out, the divider, the menu toggle and the wordmark that returns under 900px. Measured at seven widths: no dark ink on the dark ground, and under 768 the phone shell draws no bar.
- **THE COMPOSITOR IN contrast-verify WAS WRONG** and is the net for this change: it forced the result's alpha to 1, so 10% white over 10% white answered OPAQUE WHITE and reported 1:1. Over an opaque ground the new arithmetic is identical, so no older reading moves.
- **THE COLOUR CENSUS IS NOT REPRODUCIBLE**, measured: re-recorded at the unmodified parent it differs on six screens over seven values (`.ngl-empty`'s shadow, `#cal-open-reg`, `#rail-user-av`), none in this bar. So it was NOT re-recorded from a fresh sweep — my own difference was taken against the PARENT's census in the same conditions (nine values, every one the bar's) and the seven flaky lines were restored by hand. 34/40 here and 34/40 at the parent.
- **NOT TOUCHED**: the rail head stays white, so the shell's top-left corner is a notch against the band. The owner's highlight covered the bar alone.
Tests: home-page-verify 1 reversed in place and asked as a RELATION (the bar equals `--nav-bg` resolved live, never a colour); contrast-verify 31/33, theme-tokens 34/40, nav-floats 67/67, laptops 21/21, keyboard-reach 40/40, white-band-and-tabs 45/45, pages-read-alike 54/54, seven-fixes 36/36, focus-mimics-document 24/24, brand-survives-refresh 14/14.

## FIVE OFF FIVE IMAGES (Young ruled 21 Sep 2026: "You have not applied my requests")

Measured in a real browser first, because the report was that none of it was built. Three were not; one was built and UNREACHABLE; one was built in the wrong place.
- **THE FILTER CHIPS WERE DEAD.** MEASURED: a chip drew 93x30 and its `<select>` 22x28 in `rgba(0,0,0,0)`, and the centre of the chip hit-tested to `SPAN.reg-f-l` — **a press on a `<label>` does not open a native select's menu**, so four of five filters could only be opened by finding an unmarked 22px strip at the right wall. A rule had shrunk the resting control so the chip would read as a word. **THE SELECT NOW COVERS THE CHIP AND IS INVISIBLE (`.reg-chip-sel`, `position:absolute;inset:0;opacity:0`), AND THE WORD CARRIES WHAT IS CHOSEN** (`selChosen`, ONE reading, so face and control cannot disagree). Nothing shouts `!important`: the builder stopped putting `selStyle` — a whole dropdown's inline dress — on it, which is what the old rules had to shout at. **NOTHING DECORATIVE TAKES THE PRESS** (`pointer-events:none` on the funnel, the word and the caret — a press on the caret landed on the label). **AND THE CATEGORY CHIP WAS A SECOND COPY OF THE MARKUP**, so seven were fixed and it was not; it goes through `selFilter` now — THE CLOTHES FOLLOW THE BUILDER.
- **`REG_VIEWS` loses expiring60 and expiring30**; 90 stays (it is `RENEWAL_WINDOW_DAYS`). **THE FILTERS STAY in `regFiltered`** — the optional Home tiles are doors onto those cuts. Nothing is lit when one is in force and Clear says the list is narrowed.
- **CONDENSED IS DELETED** from `REG_DENSITY`; the segment reads `Object.keys(REG_DENSITY)`, never a typed list. It was the one rung that took the kind and the round off the title, so `twoLine` is gone — with the rung deleted it would be A GUARD THAT IS ALWAYS TRUE. An unknown stored value already falls back to compact: no migration, driven and proved.
- **THE HORIZON GAINED THE ARTIFACT'S DECISION COLUMN** (`300px minmax(0,1fr) 150px` on the ruler AND the row). `calHorizonDecision(r)` BORROWS everything — `renewalDecisionOf` (the one predicate every nag asks) carries the word and the day, the deadline is the nip already drawn — and computes no date. Three states: decided reads quietly, open is amber at the strong weight (the only state here that is WORK), nothing-to-say is faintest. The day prints through `regDotDate`; **`fmtDDay` is not published** and a first draft printed a raw ISO string.
- **THE LIT HALF FILLS ITS BUTTON.** `.cal-seg` stood 28px with a 16.8px fill and 5.6px of page above and below — `align-items:center` sized each half to its own line box. **STRETCH + `overflow:hidden` + `height:100%`**, which is `.doc-read-seg`'s own mechanism (19 Sep), never a second set of heights. THE SWEEP IS REAL: 16 controls across six pages; the calendar's two were the only ones leaking.
- **EVERY DIALOG FRAME CARRIES A 3px RULE** (`DLG_TOPBAR`, ONE declaration, three frames; danger wears `--danger`). A BACKGROUND, not an element — no markup, no layout, clips to the corner by itself. Written into the INLINE style because the frame states its own `background` shorthand there and a sheet rule would lose while looking correct.
- **HOME'S PREPARED ROW PUTS ITS VERBS AT THE RIGHT** (93px -> 53px; `.hm-row.is-desk` flex, acts `margin-left:auto`, wraps at a narrow card). SCOPED to `.is-desk`: the auto-triage row shares the class names, is a different surface and keeps its stack. The English button said **Discard** where the Swedish has always said "Lagg undan" and the confirm says nothing is deleted — both books say "Put away" now.
Tests: f346 (17 claims, 16 red at the parent), five-images-verify (27 checks, 21 red); f240 (5), f274, row-density-verify (its whole condensed section DRIVES the rung and now drives compact), contracts-page-verify 12d/12f re-pointed in place (they read the ink off the CONTROL, which is invisible by design; a reader's ink is on the face).

## THE SECOND FIVE IMAGES, AND THE NAME, THE TAG AND THE HORIZON (Young ruled 21 Sep 2026)

Measured against `prototype/hati-redesign-reference.html` rendered SIDE BY SIDE with HaTi before a line moved.
- **ONE DAY PRINTER, AND IT IS THE ARTIFACT'S SHAPE.** MEASURED: the artifact prints `30 Jun 2027` in the figure face at the label size and HaTi printed `30.06.2027` in the body face. `regDotDate` is the one function both register columns already went through, so both moved together and no screen carries two spellings of a day. **THE MONTH FOLLOWS THE LANGUAGE** (`langLocale()`, never `jxLocale`, never a hand-written English name — Swedish reads `30 juni 2027`). **AND IT REFUSES RATHER THAN PRINTING NaN** — the same NaN was reported 13 and 17 Sep at two of four call sites; this is the one printer now. `.reg-day` is the one class both cells wear.
- **INSIGHTS SITS DIRECTLY AFTER HOME** — markup order and nothing else, reversing 19 Sep's "after Obligations" (whose reasoning is kept beside it). **Comfortable is "Cozy"**, the artifact's own word.
- **PROVENANCE IS NOT A DESCRIPTION.** `Converted from X.docx (original stored: f_…)` was written into the one column every card prints as a DESCRIPTION. **THE ROUTE'S FIX IS THE REAL ONE** (POST /api/templates/upload writes `''`; where a document came from is `origin`/`source_type` and `templateProvenanceHtml`), and `naCardSub` refuses the shape for every row on file, falling back to the CATEGORY — a fact off the record, never an invented summary. `naCardHint` puts the provenance on the hover. **THE SENTENCE SLOT IS ALWAYS DRAWN** and reserves two lines: a grid stretches every cell to the tallest, so a clamp alone caps the long one and still lets an empty card shrink. The describe box is a `<textarea>` (same id, same cap; Enter presses Find, Shift+Enter is a newline).
- **`triageWhy` IS THE ONE READING OF WHY A READING DID NOT HAPPEN** — the four kinds the server's error already carries (noKey · rateLimit · spendCap · provider) worded for a tile, the technical sentence on the hover (`whyRaw`, `hint`). **EVERY step catch asks `triageFail`**: four printed a raw message and one was reported. **`contractOpenFieldNames`** (js/blanks.js, published) names the open fields for BOTH kinds of contract, and `tplFormOpenCount` is derived from `tplFormOpenFields` rather than written twice.
- **THE "WHAT IT FOUND" COLOUR IS THE RESULT, NOT WHETHER IT RAN** — `1 departure`, `1 still open` and `0 found` all drew GREEN. `OV_READ_TONE` gained `look:'amber'` and `bad:'ruby'`; each row works its own out beside the figure. Bold, no rules across the card, the Open links are `.ui-btn`.
- **THE NEGOTIATE HEAD DRAWS NO LINE** (`box-shadow:inset 0 -1px` off `.redline-page #ws-head`; the room's own band never drew one) and **the TERM says its length once** (`termCell=term`; `ct_fact_days` STALE on that row).
- **A CLAUSE'S NAME IS NOT PART OF ITS WORDING.** `pbDropRepeatedHeading` at the TOP of `pbFitWording` — the one reading every proposal goes through. It REFUSES rather than guesses: the WHOLE heading must match, folded free of its number and case; a one-word heading is never cut; words must be left after the cut. THE PROMPT IS NOT THE WALL (it gained the sentence anyway).
- **EVERY PREPARED ROW CARRIES ITS KIND** (`hm-dk-tag`: Notice · Read · Memo · Chase) taking the ROW's own tone, never a second table.
- **A HIDDEN CONTROL MAY NOT COLOUR ITS OWN CHOICES.** `color:transparent` on `.reg-chip-sel` was inherited by the `<option>`s in the NATIVE popup and drew a column of blank rows. `opacity:0` hides the box only; the options state their own ink and surface.
- **THE HORIZON IS THE ARTIFACT'S**: the five bands LEAD, the caption bar is gone (`cal_hz_title`/`cal_hz_head` STALE), the months read as a mono ruler rather than twelve uppercase heads with a box each, the label is who · reference · value. **THE TABLE IS THE SCROLLER** — sticky is relative to the nearest SCROLLING ancestor, so while the page scrolled the header travelled with the rows.
- **AND THE BAR IS A PILL, WITH A CUT RIGHT END WHERE IT RUNS PAST THE RULER** (Young, over two renders: *"These bars are not the same. They do not have round endings in the end"*). MEASURED: every corner of `.cal-hz-bar` computed `0px`. **PIN THE RELATION, NOT THE NUMBER** — the artifact's `4px` is half its own 8px bar, so copying the 4 onto HaTi's 14px bar copies a shallow corner rather than the shape; `999px` is this product's own "as round as this box can be" (`.rounded-full`'s rule) and follows the bar if the bar is retuned. The cut end is the artifact's rule too (`4px 0 0 4px` on an over-run): a pill says *it ends here*, a cut end says *this is where the PICTURE stops, not the agreement*. **ONE READING, TWO READERS** — the flag is the row builder's own `beyond`, which the note under the bar already prints, so the shape and the words cannot disagree. Said out loud and NOT changed: `calHorizonPos` has no floor, so a contract expiring today draws a bar of zero width; and HaTi's 2px `.cal-hz-end` tick (which the artifact does not draw) now stands a little clear of a rounded cap — kept, because it marks the exact expiry day a pill makes vaguer.
Tests: f347 (16 claims, all red at the parent), f348 (10, 9 red), five-images-two-verify (26 checks, 23 red — the parent prints `30.06.2027` in both languages, `NaN.NaN.NaN`, an INPUT at 32px, one green in every found cell, `30.06.2027 · 281 days` and the head's `inset` shadow), name-tag-horizon-verify (18 checks, 11 red — section 4 prints the ends report back: `tl/bl/tr/br` all `0px`, and `null` for the over-run shape, there being none at the parent), f348 (5) (4 claims, 4 red).
